Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any deep module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 608. The projective resolution has length at most 61 with Betti numbers bounded by 1.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_608(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 608. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^61(S, F) is trivial. QED.
